</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="p">})</span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div><h3 id="trace-log-correlation-nodejs">Trace and log correlation </h3><p>Trace and log correlation is automatic. When you emit a log using the OTel Logs API within an active Datadog trace, the <code>trace_id</code> and <code>span_id</code> are automatically added to the log record.</p><div class="code-snippet-wrapper"><div class="code-filename-wrapper d-flex justify-content-end"></div><div class="code-snippet "><div class="code-button-wrapper position-absolute"><button class="btn text-primary js-copy-button">Copy</button></div><div class="cdoc-code-snippet cdoc-language-javascript"><pre tabindex="0" class="chroma"><code><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1">// Tracer must be initialized first
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span><span class="nx">require</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s1">&#39;dd-trace&#39;</span><span class="p">).</span><span class="nx">init</span><span class="p">()</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kr">const</span> <span class="p">{</span> <span class="nx">logs</span> <span class="p">}</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nx">require</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s1">&#39;@opentelemetry/api-logs&#39;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kr">const</span> <span class="nx">express</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nx">require</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s1">&#39;express&#39;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kr">const</span> <span class="nx">app</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nx">express</span><span class="p">()</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kr">const</span> <span class="nx">logger</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nx">logs</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nx">getLogger</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s1">&#39;my-service&#39;</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s1">&#39;1.0.0&#39;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nx">app</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nx">get</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s1">&#39;/api/users/:id&#39;</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="nx">req</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="nx">res</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="p">=&gt;</span> <span class="p">{</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">  <span class="c1">// This log is automatically correlated with the &#39;express.request&#39; span
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span>  <span class="nx">logger</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nx">emit</span><span class="p">({</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="nx">severityText</span><span class="o">:</span> <span class="s1">&#39;INFO&#39;</span><span class="p">,</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="nx">severityNumber</span><span class="o">:</span> <span class="mi">9</span><span class="p">,</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="nx">body</span><span class="o">:</span> <span class="sb">`Processing user request for ID: </span><span class="si">${</span><span class="nx">req</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nx">params</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nx">id</span><span class="si">}</span><span class="sb">`</span><span class="p">,</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">  <span class="p">})</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">  <span class="nx">res</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nx">json</span><span class="p">({</span> <span class="nx">id</span><span class="o">:</span> <span class="nx">req</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nx">params</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nx">id</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="nx">name</span><span class="o">:</span> <span class="s1">&#39;John Doe&#39;</span> <span class="p">})</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="p">})</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nx">app</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nx">listen</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">3000</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is Python" data-if=cdocs-ref-85><p>The Datadog SDK supports the OpenTelemetry Logs API for Python's built-in <code>logging</code> module. You do not need to change your existing logging code.</p><h3 id="standard-logging-python">Standard logging </h3><p>This example shows a standard log message. With <code>DD_LOGS_OTEL_ENABLED=true</code>, this log is automatically captured, formatted as OTLP, and exported.</p><div class="code-snippet-wrapper"><div class="code-filename-wrapper d-flex justify-content-end"></div><div class="code-snippet "><div class="code-button-wrapper position-absolute"><button class="btn text-primary js-copy-button">Copy</button></div><div class="cdoc-code-snippet cdoc-language-python"><pre tabindex="0" class="chroma"><code><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kn">import</span> <span class="nn">logging</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kn">import</span> <span class="nn">time</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"># Get a logger</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">logger</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">logging</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">getLogger</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="vm">__name__</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">logger</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">setLevel</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">logging</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">INFO</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"># Add a handler to see logs in the console (optional)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">handler</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">logging</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">StreamHandler</span><span class="p">()</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">logger</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">addHandler</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">handler</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"># This log will be exported via OTLP</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">logger</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">info</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">&#34;This is a standard log message.&#34;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div><h3 id="trace-log-correlation-python">Trace and log correlation </h3><p>This example shows how logs emitted within an active Datadog span are automatically correlated.</p><div class="code-snippet-wrapper"><div class="code-filename-wrapper d-flex justify-content-end"></div><div class="code-snippet "><div class="code-button-wrapper position-absolute"><button class="btn text-primary js-copy-button">Copy</button></div><div class="cdoc-code-snippet cdoc-language-python"><pre tabindex="0" class="chroma"><code><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kn">from</span> <span class="nn">ddtrace</span> <span class="kn">import</span> <span class="n">tracer</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kn">import</span> <span class="nn">logging</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kn">import</span> <span class="nn">time</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"># Standard logging setup</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">logger</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">logging</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">getLogger</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="vm">__name__</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">logger</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">setLevel</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">logging</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">INFO</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">handler</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">logging</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">StreamHandler</span><span class="p">()</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">handler</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">setFormatter</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">logging</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">Formatter</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s1">&#39;</span><span class="si">%(message)s</span><span class="s1">&#39;</span><span class="p">))</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">logger</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">addHandler</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">handler</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nd">@tracer.wrap</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">&#34;do.work&#34;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">def</span> <span class="nf">do_work</span><span class="p">():</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="c1"># This log is automatically correlated with the &#39;do.work&#39; span</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="n">logger</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">info</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">&#34;This log is correlated to the active span.&#34;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="n">time</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">sleep</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mf">0.1</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="n">logger</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">warning</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">&#34;So is this one.&#34;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nb">print</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">&#34;Starting work...&#34;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">do_work</span><span class="p">()</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nb">print</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">&#34;Work complete.&#34;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is Go" data-if=cdocs-ref-86><h3 id="standard-logging-go">Standard logging </h3><p>After the Datadog SDK is initialized, you can use the standard OpenTelemetry Logs API to emit log records.</p><div class="code-snippet-wrapper"><div class="code-filename-wrapper d-flex justify-content-end"></div><div class="code-snippet "><div class="code-button-wrapper position-absolute"><button class="btn text-primary js-copy-button">Copy</button></div><div class="cdoc-code-snippet cdoc-language-go"><pre tabindex="0" class="chroma"><code><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kn">import</span> <span class="p">(</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="s">&#34;context&#34;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="s">&#34;github.com/DataDog/dd-trace-go/v2/ddtrace/opentelemetry/log&#34;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="s">&#34;go.opentelemetry.io/otel&#34;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="nx">otellog</span> <span class="s">&#34;go.opentelemetry.io/otel/log&#34;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1">// Initialize the logger provider (typically done once at startup)
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span><span class="nx">err</span> <span class="o">:=</span> <span class="nx">log</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">InitGlobalLoggerProvider</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nx">context</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">Background</span><span class="p">())</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">if</span> <span class="nx">err</span> <span class="o">!=</span> <span class="kc">nil</span> <span class="p">{</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="nb">panic</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nx">err</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="p">}</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nx">otel</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">SetLoggerProvider</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nx">log</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">GetGlobalLoggerProvider</span><span class="p">())</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1">// Get a logger
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span><span class="nx">logger</span> <span class="o">:=</span> <span class="nx">otel</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">GetLoggerProvider</span><span class="p">().</span><span class="nf">Logger</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;my-service&#34;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1">// Create and emit a log record
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span><span class="kd">var</span> <span class="nx">logRecord</span> <span class="nx">otellog</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nx">Record</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nx">logRecord</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">SetBody</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nx">otellog</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">StringValue</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;User clicked the checkout button&#34;</span><span class="p">))</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nx">logRecord</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">SetSeverity</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nx">otellog</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nx">SeverityInfo</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nx">logRecord</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">SetSeverityText</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;INFO&#34;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nx">logRecord</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">AddAttributes</span><span class="p">(</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="nx">otellog</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">String</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;cart.id&#34;</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s">&#34;c-12345&#34;</span><span class="p">),</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="nx">otellog</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">String</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;user.id&#34;</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s">&#34;u-54321&#34;</span><span class="p">),</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nx">logger</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">Emit</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nx">context</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">Background</span><span class="p">(),</span> <span class="nx">logRecord</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div><h3 id="trace-log-correlation-go">Trace and log correlation </h3><p>Trace and log correlation is automatic. When you emit a log using the OTel Logs API within an active Datadog trace, the <code>trace_id</code> and <code>span_id</code> are automatically added to the log record.</p><div class="code-snippet-wrapper"><div class="code-filename-wrapper d-flex justify-content-end"></div><div class="code-snippet "><div class="code-button-wrapper position-absolute"><button class="btn text-primary js-copy-button">Copy</button></div><div class="cdoc-code-snippet cdoc-language-go"><pre tabindex="0" class="chroma"><code><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kn">import</span> <span class="p">(</span>

</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="s">&#34;github.com/DataDog/dd-trace-go/v2/ddtrace/opentelemetry/log&#34;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="s">&#34;go.opentelemetry.io/otel&#34;</span>

</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1">// Initialize logger (typically done once at startup)
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span><span class="nx">err</span> <span class="o">:=</span> <span class="nx">log</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">InitGlobalLoggerProvider</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nx">context</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">Background</span><span class="p">())</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">if</span> <span class="nx">err</span> <span class="o">!=</span> <span class="kc">nil</span> <span class="p">{</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="nb">panic</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nx">err</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="p">}</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nx">otel</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">SetLoggerProvider</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nx">log</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">GetGlobalLoggerProvider</span><span class="p">())</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1">// Get tracer and logger
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span><span class="nx">tracer</span> <span class="o">:=</span> <span class="nx">otel</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">Tracer</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;my-service&#34;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nx">logger</span> <span class="o">:=</span> <span class="nx">otel</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">GetLoggerProvider</span><span class="p">().</span><span class="nf">Logger</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;my-service&#34;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1">// Start a span
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span><span class="nx">ctx</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="nx">span</span> <span class="o">:=</span> <span class="nx">tracer</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">Start</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nx">context</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">Background</span><span class="p">(),</span> <span class="s">&#34;process.user.request&#34;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">defer</span> <span class="nx">span</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">End</span><span class="p">()</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1">// Create and emit a log record that will be automatically correlated with the active span
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span><span class="kd">var</span> <span class="nx">logRecord</span> <span class="nx">otellog</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nx">Record</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nx">logRecord</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">SetBody</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nx">otellog</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">StringValue</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;Processing user request for ID: 12345&#34;</span><span class="p">))</span>

</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nx">logRecord</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">SetSeverityText</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;INFO&#34;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nx">logger</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="nf">Emit</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nx">ctx</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="nx">logRecord</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is Rust" data-if=cdocs-ref-87><h3 id="standard-logging-rust">Standard logging </h3><p>After the Datadog SDK is initialized, you can use the standard OpenTelemetry Logs API to emit log records.</p><div class="code-snippet-wrapper"><div class="code-filename-wrapper d-flex justify-content-end"></div><div class="code-snippet "><div class="code-button-wrapper position-absolute"><button class="btn text-primary js-copy-button">Copy</button></div><div class="cdoc-code-snippet cdoc-language-rust"><pre tabindex="0" class="chroma"><code><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">use</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">opentelemetry</span>::<span class="n">global</span><span class="p">;</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="k">use</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">opentelemetry</span>::<span class="n">logs</span>::<span class="p">{</span><span class="n">Logger</span><span class="p">,</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">LogRecord</span><span class="p">,</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">Severity</span><span class="p">};</span><span class="w">

</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="c1">// Initialize logs (typically done once at startup)
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span><span class="kd">let</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">logger_provider</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">datadog_opentelemetry</span>::<span class="n">logs</span><span class="p">().</span><span class="n">init</span><span class="p">();</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="n">global</span>::<span class="n">set_logger_provider</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">logger_provider</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">clone</span><span class="p">());</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="c1">// Get a logger
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span><span class="kd">let</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">logger</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">global</span>::<span class="n">logger_provider</span><span class="p">().</span><span class="n">logger</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;my-service&#34;</span><span class="p">);</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="c1">// Emit a log record
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span><span class="kd">let</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="k">mut</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">log_record</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">LogRecord</span>::<span class="n">default</span><span class="p">();</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="n">log_record</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">set_body</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;User clicked the checkout button&#34;</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">into</span><span class="p">());</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="n">log_record</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">set_severity_number</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">Severity</span>::<span class="n">Info</span><span class="p">);</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="n">log_record</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">set_severity_text</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;INFO&#34;</span><span class="p">);</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="n">log_record</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">add_attribute</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">KeyValue</span>::<span class="n">new</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;cart.id&#34;</span><span class="p">,</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="s">&#34;c-12345&#34;</span><span class="p">));</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="n">log_record</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">add_attribute</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">KeyValue</span>::<span class="n">new</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;user.id&#34;</span><span class="p">,</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="s">&#34;u-54321&#34;</span><span class="p">));</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="n">logger</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">emit</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">log_record</span><span class="p">);</span><span class="w">

When you emit a log using the OTel Logs API within an active Datadog trace, the <code>trace_id</code> and <code>span_id</code> are automatically added to the log record.</p><div class="code-snippet-wrapper"><div class="code-filename-wrapper d-flex justify-content-end"></div><div class="code-snippet "><div class="code-button-wrapper position-absolute"><button class="btn text-primary js-copy-button">Copy</button></div><div class="cdoc-code-snippet cdoc-language-rust"><pre tabindex="0" class="chroma"><code><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">use</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">opentelemetry</span>::<span class="n">global</span><span class="p">;</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="k">use</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">opentelemetry</span>::<span class="n">logs</span>::<span class="p">{</span><span class="n">Logger</span><span class="p">,</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">LogRecord</span><span class="p">,</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">Severity</span><span class="p">};</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="k">use</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">opentelemetry</span>::<span class="n">trace</span>::<span class="p">{</span><span class="n">Tracer</span><span class="p">,</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">TracerProvider</span><span class="p">};</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="c1">// Initialize logs and traces (typically done once at startup)
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span><span class="kd">let</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">logger_provider</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">datadog_opentelemetry</span>::<span class="n">logs</span><span class="p">().</span><span class="n">init</span><span class="p">();</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="n">global</span>::<span class="n">set_logger_provider</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">logger_provider</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">clone</span><span class="p">());</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="c1">// Get tracer and logger
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span><span class="kd">let</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">tracer</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">global</span>::<span class="n">tracer</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;my-service&#34;</span><span class="p">);</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="kd">let</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">logger</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">global</span>::<span class="n">logger_provider</span><span class="p">().</span><span class="n">logger</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;my-service&#34;</span><span class="p">);</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="c1">// Start a span
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span><span class="kd">let</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">span</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">tracer</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">start</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;process.user.request&#34;</span><span class="p">);</span><span class="w">

</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="c1">// This log is automatically correlated with the active span
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span><span class="kd">let</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="k">mut</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">log_record</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">LogRecord</span>::<span class="n">default</span><span class="p">();</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="n">log_record</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">set_body</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;Processing user request for ID: 12345&#34;</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">into</span><span class="p">());</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="n">log_record</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">set_severity_number</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">Severity</span>::<span class="n">Info</span><span class="p">);</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="n">log_record</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">set_severity_text</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s">&#34;INFO&#34;</span><span class="p">);</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w">
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="w"></span><span class="n">logger</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">emit</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">log_record</span><span class="p">);</span><span class="w">
</span></span></span></code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable" data-description="Language is Java" data-if=cdocs-ref-88><h3 id="emitting-log-java">Emitting a log </h3><p>After the Datadog SDK is initialized, you can use the standard OpenTelemetry Logs API to get a logger and emit log records.</p><div class="code-snippet-wrapper"><div class="code-filename-wrapper d-flex justify-content-end"></div><div class="code-snippet "><div class="code-button-wrapper position-absolute"><button class="btn text-primary js-copy-button">Copy</button></div><div class="cdoc-code-snippet cdoc-language-java"><pre tabindex="0" class="chroma"><code><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kn">import</span> <span class="nn">io.opentelemetry.api.GlobalOpenTelemetry</span><span class="o">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kn">import</span> <span class="nn">io.opentelemetry.api.common.AttributeKey</span><span class="o">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kn">import</span> <span class="nn">io.opentelemetry.api.logs.Logger</span><span class="o">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kn">import</span> <span class="nn">io.opentelemetry.api.logs.Severity</span><span class="o">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">Logger</span> <span class="n">logger</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">GlobalOpenTelemetry</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="na">get</span><span class="o">().</span><span class="na">getLogsBridge</span><span class="o">().</span><span class="na">get</span><span class="o">(</span><span class="s">&#34;my-service&#34;</span><span class="o">);</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">logger</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="na">logRecordBuilder</span><span class="o">()</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="o">.</span><span class="na">setBody</span><span class="o">(</span><span class="s">&#34;User clicked the checkout button.&#34;</span><span class="o">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="o">.</span><span class="na">setSeverity</span><span class="o">(</span><span class="n">Severity</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="na">INFO</span><span class="o">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="o">.</span><span class="na">setSeverityText</span><span class="o">(</span><span class="s">&#34;INFO&#34;</span><span class="o">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="o">.</span><span class="na">setAttribute</span><span class="o">(</span><span class="n">AttributeKey</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="na">stringKey</span><span class="o">(</span><span class="s">&#34;cart.id&#34;</span><span class="o">),</span> <span class="s">&#34;c-12345&#34;</span><span class="o">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="o">.</span><span class="na">setAttribute</span><span class="o">(</span><span class="n">AttributeKey</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="na">stringKey</span><span class="o">(</span><span class="s">&#34;user.id&#34;</span><span class="o">),</span> <span class="s">&#34;u-54321&#34;</span><span class="o">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="o">.</span><span class="na">emit</span><span class="o">();</span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div><h3 id="trace-log-correlation-java">Trace and log correlation </h3><p>Trace and log correlation is automatic. When you emit a log using the OTel Logs API within an active Datadog trace, the <code>trace_id</code> and <code>span_id</code> are automatically added to the log record.</p><div class="code-snippet-wrapper"><div class="code-filename-wrapper d-flex justify-content-end"></div><div class="code-snippet "><div class="code-button-wrapper position-absolute"><button class="btn text-primary js-copy-button">Copy</button></div><div class="cdoc-code-snippet cdoc-language-java"><pre tabindex="0" class="chroma"><code><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kn">import</span> <span class="nn">io.opentelemetry.api.GlobalOpenTelemetry</span><span class="o">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kn">import</span> <span class="nn">io.opentelemetry.api.logs.Logger</span><span class="o">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kn">import</span> <span class="nn">io.opentelemetry.api.logs.Severity</span><span class="o">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kn">import</span> <span class="nn">io.opentelemetry.api.trace.Span</span><span class="o">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kn">import</span> <span class="nn">io.opentelemetry.api.trace.Tracer</span><span class="o">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="kn">import</span> <span class="nn">io.opentelemetry.context.Scope</span><span class="o">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">Tracer</span> <span class="n">tracer</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">GlobalOpenTelemetry</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="na">getTracer</span><span class="o">(</span><span class="s">&#34;my-service&#34;</span><span class="o">);</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">Logger</span> <span class="n">logger</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">GlobalOpenTelemetry</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="na">get</span><span class="o">().</span><span class="na">getLogsBridge</span><span class="o">().</span><span class="na">get</span><span class="o">(</span><span class="s">&#34;my-service&#34;</span><span class="o">);</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">Span</span> <span class="n">span</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">tracer</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="na">spanBuilder</span><span class="o">(</span><span class="s">&#34;process.user.request&#34;</span><span class="o">).</span><span class="na">startSpan</span><span class="o">();</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">try</span> <span class="o">(</span><span class="n">Scope</span> <span class="n">scope</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">span</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="na">makeCurrent</span><span class="o">())</span> <span class="o">{</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="c1">// This log is automatically correlated with the active span
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span>    <span class="n">logger</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="na">logRecordBuilder</span><span class="o">()</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">        <span class="o">.</span><span class="na">setBody</span><span class="o">(</span><span class="s">&#34;Processing user request for ID: 12345&#34;</span><span class="o">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">        <span class="o">.</span><span class="na">setSeverity</span><span class="o">(</span><span class="n">Severity</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="na">INFO</span><span class="o">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">        <span class="o">.</span><span class="na">setSeverityText</span><span class="o">(</span><span class="s">&#34;INFO&#34;</span><span class="o">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">        <span class="o">.</span><span class="na">emit</span><span class="o">();</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="o">}</span> <span class="k">finally</span> <span class="o">{</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="n">span</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="na">end</span><span class="o">();</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="o">}</span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is Ruby" data-if=cdocs-ref-89><p>After the Datadog SDK is initialized, use the OpenTelemetry Logs API to get a logger and emit log records.</p><h3 id="emitting-log-ruby">Emitting a log </h3><div class="code-snippet-wrapper"><div class="code-filename-wrapper d-flex justify-content-end"></div><div class="code-snippet "><div class="code-button-wrapper position-absolute"><button class="btn text-primary js-copy-button">Copy</button></div><div class="cdoc-code-snippet cdoc-language-ruby"><pre tabindex="0" class="chroma"><code><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">logger</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="no">OpenTelemetry</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">logger_provider</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">logger</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">name</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="s1">&#39;my-service&#39;</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="ss">version</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="s1">&#39;1.0.0&#39;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="n">logger</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">on_emit</span><span class="p">(</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">  <span class="ss">severity_text</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="s1">&#39;INFO&#39;</span><span class="p">,</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">  <span class="ss">severity_number</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="mi">9</span><span class="p">,</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">  <span class="ss">body</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="s1">&#39;User clicked the checkout button.&#39;</span><span class="p">,</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">  <span class="ss">attributes</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="p">{</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="s1">&#39;cart.id&#39;</span> <span class="o">=&gt;</span> <span class="s1">&#39;c-12345&#39;</span><span class="p">,</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="s1">&#39;user.id&#39;</span> <span class="o">=&gt;</span> <span class="s1">&#39;u-54321&#39;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">  <span class="p">}</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div><h3 id="trace-log-correlation-ruby">Trace and log correlation </h3><p>Trace and log correlation is automatic. When you emit a log using the OTel Logs API within an active Datadog trace, the <code>trace_id</code> and <code>span_id</code> are added to the log record.</p><div class="code-snippet-wrapper"><div class="code-filename-wrapper d-flex justify-content-end"></div><div class="code-snippet "><div class="code-button-wrapper position-absolute"><button class="btn text-primary js-copy-button">Copy</button></div><div class="cdoc-code-snippet cdoc-language-ruby"><pre tabindex="0" class="chroma"><code><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="no">Datadog</span><span class="o">::</span><span class="no">Tracing</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">trace</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s1">&#39;do.work&#39;</span><span class="p">)</span> <span class="k">do</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">  <span class="n">logger</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="no">OpenTelemetry</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">logger_provider</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">logger</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nb">name</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="s1">&#39;my-service&#39;</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="ss">version</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="s1">&#39;1.0.0&#39;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">  <span class="n">logger</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">on_emit</span><span class="p">(</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="ss">severity_text</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="s1">&#39;INFO&#39;</span><span class="p">,</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="ss">severity_number</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="mi">9</span><span class="p">,</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="ss">body</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="s1">&#39;This log is correlated to the active span.&#39;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">  <span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">  <span class="nb">sleep</span> <span class="mi">0</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="mi">1</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">  <span class="n">logger</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">on_emit</span><span class="p">(</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="ss">severity_text</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="s1">&#39;WARN&#39;</span><span class="p">,</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="ss">severity_number</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="mi">13</span><span class="p">,</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="ss">body</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="s1">&#39;So is this one.&#39;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">  <span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">end</span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is PHP" data-if=cdocs-ref-90><p>After the Datadog SDK is initialized, use the OpenTelemetry Logs API to get a logger and emit log records.</p><h3 id="emitting-log-php">Emit a log </h3><div class="code-snippet-wrapper"><div class="code-filename-wrapper d-flex justify-content-end"></div><div class="code-snippet "><div class="code-button-wrapper position-absolute"><button class="btn text-primary js-copy-button">Copy</button></div><div class="cdoc-code-snippet cdoc-language-php"><pre tabindex="0" class="chroma"><code><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">use</span> <span class="nx">OpenTelemetry\API\Globals</span><span class="p">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">use</span> <span class="nx">OpenTelemetry\API\Logs\LogRecord</span><span class="p">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">use</span> <span class="nx">OpenTelemetry\API\Logs\Severity</span><span class="p">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1">// dd-trace-php automatically configures the LoggerProvider
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span><span class="nv">$logger</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nx">Globals</span><span class="o">::</span><span class="na">loggerProvider</span><span class="p">()</span><span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">getLogger</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s1">&#39;my-service&#39;</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="s1">&#39;1.0.0&#39;</span><span class="p">);</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nv">$record</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="k">new</span> <span class="nx">LogRecord</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s1">&#39;User clicked the checkout button.&#39;</span><span class="p">))</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">setSeverityNumber</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nx">Severity</span><span class="o">::</span><span class="na">INFO</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">setSeverityText</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s1">&#39;INFO&#39;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">setAttributes</span><span class="p">([</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">        <span class="s1">&#39;cart.id&#39;</span> <span class="o">=&gt;</span> <span class="s1">&#39;c-12345&#39;</span><span class="p">,</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">        <span class="s1">&#39;user.id&#39;</span> <span class="o">=&gt;</span> <span class="s1">&#39;u-54321&#39;</span><span class="p">,</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="p">]);</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nv">$logger</span><span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">emit</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nv">$record</span><span class="p">);</span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div><h3 id="monolog-bridge-php">Bridge Monolog to OTel logs </h3><p>To export logs written with Monolog through the OTel pipeline, attach the OpenTelemetry Monolog handler to your logger. When you attach this handler, the Datadog SDK skips its standard <code>dd.*</code> log injection on that logger. Trace correlation is instead carried in the OTLP log record's <code>trace_id</code> and <code>span_id</code> fields.</p><div class="code-snippet-wrapper"><div class="code-filename-wrapper d-flex justify-content-end"></div><div class="code-snippet "><div class="code-button-wrapper position-absolute"><button class="btn text-primary js-copy-button">Copy</button></div><div class="cdoc-code-snippet cdoc-language-php"><pre tabindex="0" class="chroma"><code><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">use</span> <span class="nx">Monolog\Logger</span><span class="p">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">use</span> <span class="nx">OpenTelemetry\API\Globals</span><span class="p">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">use</span> <span class="nx">OpenTelemetry\Contrib\Logs\Monolog\Handler</span><span class="p">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nv">$loggerProvider</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nx">Globals</span><span class="o">::</span><span class="na">loggerProvider</span><span class="p">();</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nv">$handler</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="k">new</span> <span class="nx">Handler</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nv">$loggerProvider</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="nx">Logger</span><span class="o">::</span><span class="na">INFO</span><span class="p">);</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nv">$monolog</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="k">new</span> <span class="nx">Logger</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s1">&#39;my-service&#39;</span><span class="p">);</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nv">$monolog</span><span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">pushHandler</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nv">$handler</span><span class="p">);</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nv">$monolog</span><span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">info</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s1">&#39;User clicked the checkout button.&#39;</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="p">[</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="s1">&#39;cart.id&#39;</span> <span class="o">=&gt;</span> <span class="s1">&#39;c-12345&#39;</span><span class="p">,</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="s1">&#39;user.id&#39;</span> <span class="o">=&gt;</span> <span class="s1">&#39;u-54321&#39;</span><span class="p">,</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="p">]);</span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div><p>Other Monolog handlers attached to the same logger continue to receive <code>dd.*</code> trace identifiers, so file or syslog output keeps its existing correlation behavior.</p><h3 id="trace-log-correlation-php">Correlate traces and logs </h3><p>Trace and log correlation is automatic. When you emit a log using the OTel Logs API within an active Datadog trace, the <code>trace_id</code> and <code>span_id</code> are added to the log record. To create spans with the OpenTelemetry tracing API, also set <code>DD_TRACE_OTEL_ENABLED=true</code>.</p><div class="code-snippet-wrapper"><div class="code-filename-wrapper d-flex justify-content-end"></div><div class="code-snippet "><div class="code-button-wrapper position-absolute"><button class="btn text-primary js-copy-button">Copy</button></div><div class="cdoc-code-snippet cdoc-language-php"><pre tabindex="0" class="chroma"><code><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">use</span> <span class="nx">OpenTelemetry\API\Globals</span><span class="p">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">use</span> <span class="nx">OpenTelemetry\API\Logs\LogRecord</span><span class="p">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">use</span> <span class="nx">OpenTelemetry\API\Logs\Severity</span><span class="p">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">use</span> <span class="nx">OpenTelemetry\API\Trace\SpanKind</span><span class="p">;</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nv">$tracer</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nx">Globals</span><span class="o">::</span><span class="na">tracerProvider</span><span class="p">()</span><span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">getTracer</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s1">&#39;my-service&#39;</span><span class="p">);</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nv">$logger</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nx">Globals</span><span class="o">::</span><span class="na">loggerProvider</span><span class="p">()</span><span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">getLogger</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s1">&#39;my-service&#39;</span><span class="p">);</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nv">$span</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nv">$tracer</span><span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">spanBuilder</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s1">&#39;process.user.request&#39;</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">setSpanKind</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nx">SpanKind</span><span class="o">::</span><span class="na">KIND_SERVER</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">startSpan</span><span class="p">();</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="nv">$scope</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="nv">$span</span><span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">activate</span><span class="p">();</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="k">try</span> <span class="p">{</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="c1">// This log is automatically correlated with the active span
</span></span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="c1"></span>    <span class="nv">$record</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="k">new</span> <span class="nx">LogRecord</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s1">&#39;Processing user request for ID: 12345&#39;</span><span class="p">))</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">        <span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">setSeverityNumber</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nx">Severity</span><span class="o">::</span><span class="na">INFO</span><span class="p">)</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">        <span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">setSeverityText</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s1">&#39;INFO&#39;</span><span class="p">);</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="nv">$logger</span><span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">emit</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="nv">$record</span><span class="p">);</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="p">}</span> <span class="k">finally</span> <span class="p">{</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="nv">$scope</span><span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">detach</span><span class="p">();</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl">    <span class="nv">$span</span><span class="o">-&gt;</span><span class="na">end</span><span class="p">();</span>
</span></span><span class="line"><span class="cl"><span class="p">}</span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div></div><h2 id="supported-configuration-2">Supported configuration</h2><p>To enable this feature, you must set <code>DD_LOGS_OTEL_ENABLED=true</code>.</p><p>All OTLP exporter settings (such as endpoints, protocols, and timeouts), resource attributes, and batch processor settings are configured using a shared set of OpenTelemetry environment variables.</p><p>For a complete list of all shared OTLP environment variables, see <a href="/opentelemetry/config/environment_variable_support">OpenTelemetry Environment Variables Interoperability</a>.</p><h2 id="migrate-from-other-setups-2">Migrate from other setups</h2><h3 id="existing-otel-setup-2">Existing OTel setup</h3><p>If you are already using the OpenTelemetry SDK with a manual OTLP exporter configuration, follow these steps to migrate:</p><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is .NET" data-if=cdocs-ref-91><ol><li>Add the Datadog SDK (<code>dd-trace-dotnet</code>) to your project and enable its instrumentation.</li><li>Remove any code that manually configures the <code>OtlpExporter</code> for logs. The Datadog SDK handles this configuration automatically.</li><li>Remove the <code>OpenTelemetry</code> and <code>OpenTelemetry.Exporter.OpenTelemetryProtocol</code> packages from your project's dependencies.</li><li>Set the <code>DD_LOGS_OTEL_ENABLED=true</code> environment variable.</li></ol></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is Node.js" data-if=cdocs-ref-92><ol><li>Remove the OTel SDK and OTLP Exporter packages:<div class="code-snippet-wrapper"><div class="code-filename-wrapper d-flex justify-content-end"></div><div class="code-snippet "><div class="code-button-wrapper position-absolute"><button class="btn text-primary js-copy-button">Copy</button></div><div class="cdoc-code-snippet cdoc-language-sh"><pre tabindex="0" class="chroma"><code><span class="line"><span class="cl">npm uninstall @opentelemetry/sdk-logs @opentelemetry/exporter-otlp-logs
</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div></li><li>Remove all manual OTel SDK initialization code (for example, <code>new LoggerProvider()</code>, <code>addLogRecordProcessor()</code>, <code>new OTLPLogExporter()</code>).</li><li>Install the Datadog SDK: <code>npm install dd-trace</code></li><li>Keep the <code>@opentelemetry/api-logs</code> package.</li><li>Set <code>DD_LOGS_OTEL_ENABLED=true</code> and initialize <code>dd-trace</code> at the top of your application.</li></ol><p>Your existing code that uses <code>logs.getLogger()</code> continues to work.</p></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is Python" data-if=cdocs-ref-93><ol><li>Remove your manual setup code (for example, <code>LoggerProvider</code>, <code>BatchLogRecordProcessor</code>, and <code>OTLPLogExporter</code> instantiation).</li><li>Enable <code>ddtrace-run</code> auto-instrumentation for your application.</li><li>Set the <code>DD_LOGS_OTEL_ENABLED=true</code> environment variable.</li></ol><p>The Datadog SDK programmatically configures the OTel SDK for you.</p></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is Go" data-if=cdocs-ref-94><ol><li>Add the Datadog SDK (<code>dd-trace-go/v2</code>) to your project and enable its instrumentation.</li><li>Remove any code that manually configures the <code>OTLPLogExporter</code>. The Datadog SDK handles this configuration automatically.</li><li>Remove manual <code>LoggerProvider</code> setup and replace with <code>log.InitGlobalLoggerProvider()</code>.</li><li>Set the <code>DD_LOGS_OTEL_ENABLED=true</code> environment variable.</li></ol></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is Rust" data-if=cdocs-ref-95><ol><li>Add the Datadog SDK (<code>datadog-opentelemetry</code>) to your project and enable its instrumentation.</li><li>Remove any code that manually configures the <code>OTLPLogExporter</code>. The Datadog SDK handles this configuration automatically.</li><li>Replace manual logger provider setup with <code>datadog_opentelemetry::logs().init()</code>.</li><li>Set the <code>DD_LOGS_OTEL_ENABLED=true</code> environment variable.</li></ol></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable" data-description="Language is Java" data-if=cdocs-ref-96><ol><li>Add the Datadog SDK (<code>dd-trace-java</code>) to your project and <a href="/tracing/trace_collection/dd_libraries/java/">enable its instrumentation</a>.</li><li>Make sure you only depend on the OpenTelemetry API (and not the OpenTelemetry SDK).</li><li>Set the <code>DD_LOGS_OTEL_ENABLED=true</code> environment variable.</li></ol></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is Ruby" data-if=cdocs-ref-97><ol><li>Remove your manual setup code (for example, <code>LoggerProvider</code>, <code>BatchLogRecordProcessor</code>, and <code>OTLPLogExporter</code> instantiation).</li><li>Add the Datadog SDK (<code>datadog</code>) gem and call <code>Datadog.configure</code> before <code>OpenTelemetry::SDK.configure</code>.</li><li>Set the <code>DD_LOGS_OTEL_ENABLED=true</code> environment variable.</li></ol><p>The Datadog SDK programmatically configures the OTel SDK for you.</p></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is PHP" data-if=cdocs-ref-98><ol><li><a href="/tracing/trace_collection/dd_libraries/php/#install-the-extension">Install the Datadog PHP SDK</a>.</li><li>Remove any code that manually configures the OTLP logs endpoint. The Datadog SDK automatically registers the correct endpoint with the OpenTelemetry SDK.</li><li>Set <code>DD_LOGS_OTEL_ENABLED=true</code>.</li></ol></div><h3 id="existing-datadog-log-injection">Existing Datadog log injection</h3><p>If you are using Datadog's traditional log injection (where <code>DD_LOGS_INJECTION=true</code> adds trace context to text logs) and an Agent to tail log files:</p><ol><li>Set the <code>DD_LOGS_OTEL_ENABLED=true</code> environment variable.</li><li>The Datadog SDK automatically disables the old log injection style (<code>DD_LOGS_INJECTION</code>) to prevent duplicate trace metadata in your logs. Trace correlation is handled by the structured OTLP payload.</li><li>Verify your Datadog Agent is configured to receive OTLP logs (version 7.48.0 or greater is required)</li><li>Disable any file-based log collection for this service to avoid duplicate logs.</li></ol><h2 id="troubleshooting-2">Troubleshooting</h2><ul><li>Verify <code>DD_LOGS_OTEL_ENABLED</code> is set to <code>true</code>.</li><li>Verify that your OTLP destination is configured correctly to receive logs.</li><li>If you are sending data to the Datadog Agent, verify OTLP ingestion is enabled. See <a href="/opentelemetry/setup/otlp_ingest_in_the_agent/?tab=host#enabling-otlp-ingestion-on-the-datadog-agent">Enabling OTLP Ingestion on the Datadog Agent</a> for details.</li></ul><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is .NET" data-if=cdocs-ref-99><ul><li>Verify Datadog automatic instrumentation is active. This feature relies on Datadog's automatic instrumentation to function. Verify you have completed all setup steps to enable the .NET instrumentation hooks, as these are required to intercept the log data.</li></ul></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is Node.js" data-if=cdocs-ref-100><ul><li>Verify <code>dd-trace</code> is initialized first. The Datadog SDK must be initialized at the top of your application, <em>before</em> any other modules are imported.</li><li>Verify <code>@opentelemetry/api-logs</code> is installed. The Node.js SDK requires this API package.</li></ul></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is Python" data-if=cdocs-ref-101><ul><li>Verify <code>opentelemetry-sdk</code> is installed. The Python SDK requires <code>opentelemetry-sdk</code> and <code>opentelemetry-exporter-otlp</code> to be installed in your Python environment.</li><li>Verify <code>ddtrace-run</code> is active. Verify that you are running your application with <code>ddtrace-run</code> (or have imported and initialized <code>ddtrace</code> manually).</li></ul></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is Go" data-if=cdocs-ref-102><ul><li>Verify <code>DD_LOGS_OTEL_ENABLED=true</code> is set. Logs are disabled by default in dd-trace-go.</li><li>Verify the Datadog SDK is imported and initialized: <code>import &quot;github.com/DataDog/dd-trace-go/v2/ddtrace/opentelemetry/log&quot;</code></li><li>Verify <code>log.InitGlobalLoggerProvider()</code> is called before using the logger.</li></ul></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is Rust" data-if=cdocs-ref-103><ul><li>Verify <code>DD_LOGS_OTEL_ENABLED=true</code> is set. Logs are disabled by default.</li><li>Verify that a transport feature (<code>logs-grpc</code> or <code>logs-http</code>) is enabled in your <code>Cargo.toml</code> file.</li><li>Verify <code>datadog_opentelemetry::logs().init()</code> is called before using the logger.</li><li>Check protocol configuration. Only <code>grpc</code> and <code>http/protobuf</code> protocols are supported. HTTP/JSON is not supported.</li></ul></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable" data-description="Language is Java" data-if=cdocs-ref-104><ul><li>Verify the <code>dd-trace-java</code> javaagent is running. The javaagent registers itself as the global OTel LoggerProvider at startup; without it, OTel API calls fall through to no-op providers and no data is sent.</li></ul></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is Ruby" data-if=cdocs-ref-105><ul><li>Verify required gems (<code>opentelemetry-logs-sdk</code> and <code>opentelemetry-exporter-otlp-logs</code>) are installed in your Ruby environment.</li><li>Verify <code>Datadog.configure</code> is called before <code>OpenTelemetry::SDK.configure</code>.</li><li>Verify you are using <code>OpenTelemetry.logger_provider</code>, not Ruby's stdlib <code>Logger</code> or <code>OpenTelemetry.logger</code>.</li></ul></div><div class="cdoc__toggleable cdoc__hidden" data-description="Language is PHP" data-if=cdocs-ref-106><ul><li>Verify that <code>open-telemetry/sdk</code> (1.0.0 or later) and <code>open-telemetry/exporter-otlp</code> are installed.</li><li>If <code>OpenTelemetry\API\Globals::loggerProvider()</code> returns a no-op provider, the <code>LoggerProvider</code> was never registered. Set <code>OTEL_PHP_AUTOLOAD_ENABLED=true</code> or register it manually: <code>Sdk::builder()-&gt;setLoggerProvider(...)-&gt;buildAndRegisterGlobal()</code>.</li><li>If <code>OTEL_PHP_AUTOLOAD_ENABLED=true</code> activates trace or metric exporters you didn't intend, set <code>OTEL_TRACES_EXPORTER=none</code> and <code>OTEL_METRICS_EXPORTER=none</code>.</li><li>If logs arrive but Monolog entries lack trace correlation, confirm the OpenTelemetry Monolog handler is attached to that logger.</li></ul></div></div></div><h2 id="further-reading">Further
